Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The equilibrium operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 862. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 819 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the equilibrium space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 862 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 819. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any equilibrium module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 325. The projective resolution has length at most 73 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

This completes the proof. QED.
